# LICENSE_QUARANTINE.md — GPL/AGPL quarantine manifest

Owner law: GPL/AGPL-licensed code is **quarantined** — it is NEVER linked, imported, or wired into shipping paths. It stays isolated with this manifest until a license audit clears it. No exceptions.

## Doctrine

- **Quarantine means:** the code may exist in the repo for reference/research, but no production script imports it, no build links it, no shipped artifact embeds it.
- **Tool use ≠ code reuse:** running a GPL application as a standalone tool (e.g. opening Krita to paint) does not infect our pipeline — output artwork remains ours per the Krita/GIMP GPL FAQ doctrine. The quarantine targets *code integration*, not *tool usage*.
- **Audit path:** an item leaves quarantine only after a license audit documents a compatible relicense, a clean-room replacement, or a linking exception. The audit note goes in the table below.
- **Catalog badges:** a catalog entry for a quarantined item carries either 🚫 (copyleft code — research lane only, never wired) or ✅ with an explicit standalone-tool-use / separate-process framing and a **QUARANTINED (GPL/AGPL)** status flag (the ✅ covers tool use/output only — the code stays quarantined). ❓ is reserved for licenses genuinely not yet verified — never on an entry whose license line already says "(verified)".

## Row-number convention + duplicate mapping (Wave 8 Lane B, 2026-10-07)

Renumbering would silently invalidate all of them, so duplicates stay in place as superseded rows — they are NEVER deleted or renumbered.

## Scope: weak copyleft (LGPL / MPL / EPL / CeCILL) — recommendation PENDING OWNER VERDICT

- **The doctrine as written covers GPL/AGPL only.** Two rows on this manifest sit outside that letter:
  - **Row 63 marytts — LGPL-3.0** (verified: root LICENSE.md = LGPL-3.0 text).
  - **Row 86 G'MIC — CeCILL-2.1** (verified: gmic.eu + gmic 3.2.5 release notes). CeCILL is a French GPL-compatible copyleft license — *strong* copyleft, unlike LGPL.
- **RECOMMENDATION (Wave 8 Lane B → owner verdict required): DELIST row 63 (marytts) from quarantine** and record it under a new "weak-copyleft watchlist" note instead. Reasoning: (1) LGPL-3.0 was explicitly designed to permit dynamic linking inside proprietary/commercial works — the hazard this quarantine guards against (forced open-sourcing of shipping code, AGPL network-service source disclosure) does not apply to LGPL; (2) Wave-1 precedent already kept LGPL-2.1 Pitivi OFF this manifest — keeping marytts quarantined is inconsistent with that standing convention; (3) practical risk is near zero — marytts is a Java TTS server, not a shipping candidate, and the catalog already carries permissive TTS alternatives (Kokoro, Chatterbox, KittenTTS, Orpheus-TTS); (4) the conservative posture is preserved by a standing weak-copyleft rule: LGPL/MPL/EPL-family code is *documented and audit-gated* (dynamic-link only, no static embedding, modifications shared) but does NOT get a quarantine row.
- **G'MIC stays quarantined regardless** — CeCILL is strong copyleft (GPL-compatible), so it meets the quarantine bar on substance even if not on the letter "GPL/AGPL".
- **STATUS: row 63 stays quarantined until the owner rules.** Doctrine scope is owner-level policy; this recommendation does NOT take effect unilaterally. The Watchdog/audit lanes: treat row 63 as quarantined until the owner's verdict is recorded here.
- **Weak-copyleft watchlist (documented + audit-gated — NOT quarantined; same owner verdict pending):**
  - **Faust** (grame-cncm/faust) — **LGPL-2.1-or-later** (RELICENSED: upstream root COPYING.txt is the LGPL-2.1 text with a 'FAUST compiler, Version 2.90.4' header, verified 2026-10-07 — older versions were GPL-2.0; the catalog's old 'GPL compiler' claim was stale). Catalog entry stays ✅ with generated-output framing (compiler output belongs to its author); no quarantine row.
  - **OpenAL Soft** (kcat/openal-soft) — **LGPL-2.0-or-later** per upstream README (verified Wave 8 A). Catalog entry stays ✅ with dynamic-link-only framing; no quarantine row.
  - **VapourSynth** (vapoursynth/vapoursynth) — **LGPL-2.1** per GitHub API spdx_id (verified 2026-10-07; catalog was ❓, corrected to ✅). Wire only as dynamically-linked framework; plugins carry their own licenses; no quarantine row.
- **Standing rule applied:** LGPL/MPL/EPL-family items are *documented and audit-gated* (dynamic-link only, no static embedding, modifications shared) but do NOT get quarantine rows. Row 63 (marytts) is the grandfathered exception — it STAYS quarantined until the owner rules on the delist recommendation. This watchlist documents items under the same pending owner verdict; it does not decide the verdict. **No owner verdict has been recorded as of 2026-10-07 — the question remains open.** (Re-affirmed Wave 21 Lane E, 2026-10-07: LGPL/MPL/EPL-family doctrine still resolves only on owner ruling; weak-copyleft rows stay quarantined meanwhile.)

## Notes from Wave-1 research

- Mimic3 is AGPL-3.0 (not Apache-2.0 as commonly assumed) — quarantined; use Kokoro or Piper for wired TTS.
- so-vits-svc and aeneas are AGPL-3.0 — quarantined; use RVC/Applio (MIT) and stable-ts instead.
- ComfyUI and AUTOMATIC1111 are GPL-3.0 — quarantined as backends; use InvokeAI (Apache-2.0) for the permissive local generation path.
- Synfig, Krita, Pencil2D, TupiTube, Enve, Glaxnimate, AnimeEffects, MyPaint are GPL — fine as standalone artist tools; their *code* is never integrated.
- Papagayo-NG is GPL — quarantined; Rhubarb Lip Sync (MIT) is the wired lip-sync path.
- Shotcut, Olive, Flowblade, LosslessCut, OpenShot are GPL; Video2X and FlowFrames are AGPL — quarantined; Pitivi is LGPL-2.1 and stays off this list.
- eSpeak-NG and RHVoice are GPL — quarantined; note RHVoice Lab's prebuilt *voices* are CC-BY-NC-ND — never ship those voices regardless.
- Piper CORRECTION 2026-10-07: the pip-installable `piper-tts` 1.8.0 is GPL-3.0-or-later per its own PyPI metadata (OHF-voice/piper1-gpl) — quarantined. Wired only as a separate local process, never linked. The archived rhasspy/piper MIT version is not what pip installs; do not treat any `pip install piper-tts` as MIT.
## Notes from Wave-5 research (Worker D, 2026-10-07)

- No new quarantine rows this wave (max stays at 65). Reads done: nari-labs/Dia (Apache-2.0), microsoft/VibeVoice (MIT code license — but research-only model designation), Zyphra/Zonos (Apache-2.0; org renamed ZyphraAI → Zyphra; ZONOS2 is a separate MIT model), Oculus SDK License (proprietary EULA — NOT copyleft; commercial use expressly permitted per §2.1).
- Zonos' eSpeak-NG phonemization dependency (GPL-3.0, quarantine row 7) is already covered; standalone-binary-use doctrine stands. Dia/VibeVoice are permissively licensed (no copyleft) but gated by vendor research-intent terms — documented in docs/VOICE_COMMERCIAL_USE_WAVE5.md, not here.

## Notes from Wave-3 research

- Wave 3 added 6 quarantined items (total 56): Goo Engine, Blender-StellarToon, 2D-Cel-Toon-Shader-v2-Plus, manga-image-translator, comic-text-detector (all GPL-3.0), libre-manga-translator (AGPL-3.0-or-later) — all anime/2D tooling, study-only.
- NOT quarantined (verified Wave 3): nijigenerate + Inochi Creator are BSD-2-Clause (the donation nagscreen is a prompt, not a license change); Wan 2.2 code AND weights are Apache-2.0; InvokeAI is Apache-2.0 (models run inside it carry their own licenses — SD/SDXL Stability Community License is non-commercial); sherpa-onnx is Apache-2.0; mss is MIT.
- Catalog correction Wave 3: aeneas was wrongly badged ✅ commercial-safe in two catalog lines — it is AGPL-3.0, quarantine was already correct; headers fixed.

## Notes from Wave-2 research

- Wave 2 added 23 quarantined items (total 50): whisper-timestamped is AGPL-3.0 (not MIT as assumed); Upscayl is AGPL-3.0; Kitsu is AGPL-3.0; Kdenlive/Avidemux/Cinelerra-GG/LiVES/HandBrake/VidCutter/chaiNNer join the GPL NLE/finishing quarantine; Wick Editor, LibreSprite, Inkscape, GIMP, Blender Grease Pencil/VSE, StoryPencil, StoryToolkitAI, Allosaurus, Praat, SubtitleComposer, Seed-VC, Piper (OHF-Voice), SubtitleComposer are GPL — standalone tool use only.
- NOT quarantined: FFmpeg default build is LGPL-2.1-or-later (stays off this list per Wave-1 convention); OpenGameArt is a mixed per-asset content library (CC0/CC-BY/CC-BY-SA/OGA-BY/GPL per asset) — per-asset license check required, kept as ❓ in the catalog.

## Notes from Wave-6 research (Worker A, 2026-10-07)

- Wave 6 A added 7 quarantine rows (total 84). Key correction: ADetailer is AGPL-3.0 (root LICENSE.md is the AGPL text) — NOT MIT as the task brief assumed; quarantined immediately.
- ComfyUI ecosystem split verified per-repo: ComfyUI-Manager, ComfyUI-Impact-Pack, ComfyUI-VideoHelperSuite are AGPL-3.0 (quarantined); ComfyUI-AnimateDiff-Evolved is Apache-2.0 code and ComfyUI-Frame-Interpolation is MIT code and comfyui_controlnet_aux is Apache-2.0 code — those three stay OUT of quarantine as code (they run inside the GPL-quarantined ComfyUI host, which is fine for standalone tool use).
- NOT quarantined (verified Wave 6 A): ai-toolkit (Ostris) is MIT (not AGPL as assumed); Real-CUGAN upstream is MIT (not NC); StyleTTS 2 is MIT (not CC-BY-NC-ND); F5-TTS is MIT (not CC-BY-NC); GPT-SoVITS is MIT; SadTalker is now Apache-2.0 (NC restriction removed upstream); MagicAnimate is BSD-3-Clause; ToonCrafter is Apache-2.0; MimicMotion is Apache-2.0; VALL-E X (Plachtaa) is MIT; FOMM is MIT.
- Custom non-commercial (NOT copyleft — research lane, no quarantine row needed): SUPIR, StableSR, CodeFormer (S-Lab-style NC), Fish Speech (Fish Audio Research License), AnimeGANv2/v3 (author NC license), IndexTTS-2.5 weights (bilibili Model Use License).

## Notes from Wave-5 A2 research

- Wave 5 A2 added 10 quarantined music/plugin items (total 75): Surge XT, Dexed, Helm, Odin 2 (all GPL-3.0 synths); ZynAddSubFX (GPL-2.0-or-later); LMMS, Ardour, Audacity (GPL DAWs/editor); CHOW Tape Model, Dragonfly Reverb (GPL-3.0 plugins). Free-proprietary alternatives (Vital free tier, Valhalla Supermassive, TDR tools) stay usable; only source-code integration is barred.
- NOT quarantined: Vital (free Basic tier — binary use OK; only its GPLv3 source is off-limits), Airwindows (MIT), FluidR3 (MIT), VSCO2 CE (CC0), Salamander Grand (CC-BY 3.0).

## Notes from Wave-8 Lane B quarantine reconciliation (Worker B, 2026-10-07)

- **Cross-audit of rows 85–110 (26 rows, added by the Wave 7 A catalog lane):** every claimed license was re-checked against an upstream source (README license section / release notes / package metadata / third-party license attributions — never assumed). Result: 19 confirmed as claimed, 6 license-string refinements, 1 upstream-identity correction, 1 new duplicate found. **No row was wrong enough to change quarantine standing** — all 26 are copyleft and stay quarantined.
- **Refinements (quarantine unaffected — still copyleft):**
  - Row 92 Parselmouth: GPL-3.0 → **GPL-3.0-or-later** (upstream README: "version 3 or later").
  - Row 100 SuperCollider: GPL-3.0 → **GPL-3.0-or-later** (upstream README: "version 3 … or (at your option) any later version").
  - Row 108 opensubtitles-api: GPLv3 → **GPL-3.0-or-later** (README: "either version 3 of the License, or (at your option) any later version").
  - Row 104 SPPAS: AGPL-3.0 → **AGPL-3.0-or-later**; upstream identity corrected — canonical is **brigittebigi/sppas** (v5.x, PyPI + sppas.org state AGPL-3.0-or-later); brnoliveira/sppas is a fork. Older v4.x docs were GPL-3.0 (version split documented, not a contradiction).
  - Row 105 FAVE-align: canonical repo moved JoFrhwld/new-fave → **Forced-Alignment-and-Vowel-Extraction/new-fave**; GPL-3.0 badge confirmed.
  - Row 103 TAL-NoiseMaker: GPL-2.0 re-verified via three independent GPL-2.0 attributions (DISTRHO-Ports vendored source, schwung-noisemaker, ykchorus); root LICENSE still unfetchable — forks are the documented source of truth.
- **Confirmed as claimed (upstream source re-checked 2026-10-07):** 85 essentia AGPL-3.0 (MTG/essentia README badge; dual-licensed commercial via MTG-UPF) · 87 Natron GPL-2.0 (GitHub license field + tools README "main Natron distribution is GPL2") · 89 OBS Studio GPL-2.0 · 90 VLC GPL-2.0 · 91 RawTherapee GPL-3.0 · 93 aubio GPL-3.0 (multiple third-party attributions) · 95 darktable GPL-3.0 · 96 BlenderGIS GPL-3.0 (domlysz/BlenderGIS GitHub license field) · 97 alass GPL-3.0 (kaegi/alass; multiple downstream THIRD-PARTY attributions) · 98 Bazarr GPL-3.0 · 99 Gaupol GPL-3.0 (otsaloma/gaupol GPL headers + COPYING) · 101 TidalCycles GPL-3.0 · 102 VCV Rack GPL-3.0 (licenses doc, per Wave 7 A fetch) · 106 Jubler AGPL-3.0 (teras/Jubler README "Licensed under the GNU Affero General Public License v3"; license changed GPL-2.0 → AGPL-3.0 in 2025) · 107 Gnome Subtitles GPL-2.0-or-later (RPM package metadata) · 109 CCExtractor GPL-2.0 (upstream README "GNU General Public License version 2.0").
- Rows 88 Yoshimi, 89 OBS, 90 VLC, 91 RawTherapee, 95 darktable, 98 Bazarr, 101 TidalCycles, 102 VCV Rack: no contradiction found in targeted re-checks; standing on the Wave 7 A lane's 2026-10-07 raw-LICENSE fetches. Full re-sweep of these 8 is queued for the next quarantine audit wave.
- **Duplicate resolved:** row 110 (LMMS, sfx lane) duplicates row 71 (LMMS, daw lane) — marked SUPERSEDED, see row 71. This is the 4th duplicate group (with aeneas 1/2, Seed-VC 43/58, so-vits-svc 24/65). Distinct projects: 106 (was counted 81 at the Wave-7 audit when the manifest had 84 rows).
- **Dedup decision:** append-only with the prominent dedup mapping above — NOT renumbered. docs/RESOURCE_CATALOG.md cross-references specific row numbers in ~30+ places and concurrent Wave-8 lanes are appending rows; renumbering would invalidate all of it.
- **LGPL doctrine:** recommendation written into the Scope section above — DELIST row 63 (marytts, LGPL-3.0) to a weak-copyleft watchlist, PENDING OWNER VERDICT. Row 63 stays quarantined until the owner rules. G'MIC (row 86, CeCILL-2.1) stays quarantined regardless (strong copyleft).
- **Catalog:** docs/RESOURCE_CATALOG.md "License red flags" header section rewritten to reflect current quarantine state (110 rows / 106 distinct projects, top categories, one-paragraph doctrine). No #### entries touched.

## Notes from Wave-9 Lane B quarantine re-sweep (Worker B, 2026-10-07)

- **Re-sweep of the 8 Wave-7-A rows queued by Wave 8 (rows 88, 89, 90, 91, 95, 98, 101, 102):** every claimed license re-checked against an upstream source (GitHub API license field, fetched license text, README license section — never assumed). Result: 6 confirmed as claimed, 2 license-string refinements, 1 upstream-move note. **No row was wrong enough to change quarantine standing** — all 8 are copyleft and stay quarantined.
- **Refinements (quarantine unaffected — still copyleft):**
  - Row 88 Yoshimi: GPL-2.0 → **GPL-2.0-or-later** (COPYING foreword: "confirmed as Version 2, or (at your option) any later version"; canonical repo now Yoshimi/yoshimi).
